李悝	Li Kui (455-395 BC), legalist philosopher and statesman of Wei state 魏國|魏国[Wei4 guo2]Lǐ Kuī
李懷遠	=李怀远
李成桂	Yi Seong-gye (1335-1408), founder and first king of Korean Yi dynasty (1392-1910)Lǐ Chéng guì
李成江	Li Chengjiang (1979-), Chinese figure skaterLǐ Chéng jiāng
李承晚	Syngman Rhee (1875-1965), US-trained Korean politician and dictator, president of Republic of Korea 1948-1960Lǐ Chéng wǎn
李振藩	Li Zhenfan (1940-1973), real name of the actor Bruce Lee 李小龍|李小龙[Li3 Xiao3 long2]Lǐ Zhèn fān
李政道	Tsung-Dao Lee (1926–2024), Chinese American physicist, Columbia University, 1957 Nobel laureateLǐ Zhèng dào
李敏勇	Li Minyong (1947-), Taiwanese poetLǐ Mǐn yǒng
李斯	Li Si (c. 280-208 BC), Legalist philosopher, calligrapher and prime minister of Qin kingdom and Qin dynasty from 246 to 208 BCLǐ Sī
李斯特	Ferenc (Franz) Liszt (1811-1886), Hungarian composerLǐ sī tèJoseph Lister (1883-1897), British surgeon and bacteriologistLǐ sī tè
李斯特菌	Listeria monocytogeneLǐ sī tè jūn
李旦	Li Dan, personal name of sixth Tang emperor Ruizong 唐睿宗[Tang2 Rui4 zong1] (662-716), reigned 684-690 and 710-712Lǐ Dàn
李时珍	Li Shizhen (1518-1593), Ming botanist and pharmacologist, author of Compendium of Medical Herbs 本草綱目|本草纲目[Ben3 cao3 Gang1 mu4]Lǐ Shí zhēn
李昂	Li Ang, personal name of fifteenth Tang emperor Wenzong 文宗[Wen2 zong1] (809-840), reigned 827-840Lǐ áng
李昉	Li Fang (925-996), scholar between Tang and Song dynasties, author of fictional historyLǐ Fǎng
李昌鎬	=李昌镐
李昌镐	Lee Chang-ho (1975–), South Korean professional Go playerLǐ Chāng hào
李明博	Lee Myung-bak (1941-), South Korean businessman, one-time chairman of Hyundai, president of South Korea 2008-2013Lǐ Míng bó
